 - **Direct Gaze Challenge** (Fourth Wall, Confident): Face filling the frame, nose almost touching the lens. Unwavering, intense eye contact that breaks the fourth wall, challenging the viewer with a confident stare.
 - **Flirty Upward Gaze** (Fourth Wall, Flirty, Extreme High Angle): Camera positioned just above eye level, very close distance. Looking up through the eyelashes with a soft, playful smile, creating an intimate POV of being admired. Focus sharply on the irises.

 - **Adoring Upward Look** (Fourth Wall, Romantic, Extreme High Angle): Extremely shallow depth of field. Looking sharply upward at the "viewer" with wide, sparkling eyes full of adoration, placing the viewer in a dominant perspective.
 - **Judgmental Stare** (Fourth Wall, Sassy): Sitting forward with chin resting on hands, looking over the top of sunglasses (or lowered eyelids) directly into the camera lens with a playful, assessing, and slightly haughty expression.

+class PlatformUtils:
+    """Standardized platform-specific operations (web-safe)."""
+    
+    @staticmethod
+    def open_path(path: str, is_web: bool = False):
+        """Open a file or directory in the system's default handler."""
+        if is_web:
+            return  # No-op on web
+        try:
+            if sys.platform == "win32":
+                os.startfile(path)
+            elif sys.platform == "darwin":
+                subprocess.run(["open", path])
+            else:
+                subprocess.run(["xdg-open", path])
+        except Exception as e:
+            from utils import logger
+            logger.error(f"Failed to open path {path}: {e}")
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def copy_to_clipboard(page: ft.Page, text: str, is_web: bool = False) -> bool:
+        """Copy text to clipboard. Uses Flet API (works on both web and desktop)."""
+        # Method 1: Flet API (works on web and desktop)
+        if hasattr(page, "set_clipboard"):
+            try:
+                page.set_clipboard(text)
+                return True
+            except Exception:
+                pass
+
+        # Method 2: OS-specific commands (desktop only)
+        if not is_web:
+            try:
+                if sys.platform == "win32":
+                    subprocess.run("clip", input=text, text=True, shell=True, check=True)
+                    return True
+                elif sys.platform == "darwin":
+                    subprocess.run("pbcopy", input=text, text=True, check=True)
+                    return True
+            except Exception:
+                pass
+            
+        return False

+def extract_colors(text):
+    """Extract HEX colors from text."""
+    if not isinstance(text, str):
+        return []
+    hex_pattern = r'#([A-Fa-f0-9]{6})'
+    return re.findall(hex_pattern, text.upper())
+
+def analyze_color_diversity(loader):
+    """Analyze the distribution of HEX colors in outfits."""
+    print("Analyzing Color Diversity...")
+    chars = loader.load_characters()
+    color_counts = Counter()
+    
+    for char_name, char_data in chars.items():
+        outfits = char_data.get("outfits", {})
+        for outfit_name, outfit_data in outfits.items():
+            if isinstance(outfit_data, dict):
+                # Recursively check for colors in nested dicts
+                def scan_dict(d):
+                    for k, v in d.items():
+                        if isinstance(v, dict):
+                            scan_dict(v)
+                        elif isinstance(v, str):
+                            color_counts.update(extract_colors(v))
+                        elif isinstance(v, list):
+                            for item in v:
+                                if isinstance(item, str):
+                                    color_counts.update(extract_colors(item))
+                scan_dict(outfit_data)
+    return color_counts
+
+def generate_color_report(color_counts):
+    """Generate a markdown report for color diversity."""
+    report_path = project_root / "auditing" / "reports" / "color_diversity.md"
+    print(f"Generating color report: {report_path}")
+    
+    with open(report_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+        f.write("# ≡ƒÄ¿ Color Diversity Report\n\n")
+        f.write("Distribution of HEX colors across the outfit library.\n\n")
+        
+        if not color_counts:
+            f.write("ΓÜá∩╕Å No HEX colors detected in outfits. Ensure colors are specified as `#RRGGBB`.\n")
+            return
+            
+        f.write("| Color Swatch | HEX Code | Count | Frequency |\n")
+        f.write("|---|---|---|---|\n")
+        
+        total = sum(color_counts.values())
+        for color, count in color_counts.most_common(50):
+            percent = (count / total) * 100
+            # Note: Markdown doesn't support background colors easily, so we just use text and counts
+            f.write(f"| `#{color}` | `#{color}` | {count} | {percent:.1f}% |\n")
+            
+        if len(color_counts) > 50:
+            f.write(f"\n*...and {len(color_counts) - 50} more unique colors.*")
+

+def suggest_missing_tags(content, current_tags):
+    """Scan content for keywords and suggest missing tags."""
+    suggestions = []
+    content_lower = content.lower()
+    for pattern, tag in KEYWORD_MAP.items():
+        if re.search(pattern, content_lower):
+            # If tag not already present (case-insensitive check)
+            if tag.lower() not in [t.lower() for t in current_tags]:
+                suggestions.append(tag)
+    return list(set(suggestions))

+    async def _wait_for_generation(self, page: Page, timeout_ms: int = 45000):
+        """Wait for generation to complete by watching the UI state."""
+        print(f"Waiting for generation (max {timeout_ms/1000}s)...")
+        start_time = time.time()
+        
+        while (time.time() - start_time) * 1000 < timeout_ms:
+            # Check for "Stop" button
+            stop_btn = page.locator('button[aria-label*="Stop"]').first
+            if await stop_btn.count() > 0 and await stop_btn.is_visible():
+                await page.wait_for_timeout(1000)
+                continue
+                
+            # Check for "Run" button becoming enabled
+            run_btn = page.locator('button[aria-label*="Run"]').first
+            if await run_btn.count() > 0:
+                is_disabled = await run_btn.get_attribute("disabled")
+                if is_disabled is not None:
+                    await page.wait_for_timeout(1000)
+                    continue
+            
+            # Check for error messages or safety blocks
+            if await page.locator("text='Safety'").count() > 0 or await page.locator("text='blocked'").count() > 0:
+                print("Detected potential safety block or error.")
+                break
+
+            await page.wait_for_timeout(2000)
+            break
+            
+        print(f"Generation finished or timed out after {int(time.time() - start_time)}s")

+    def ensure_character_loaded(self, name: str) -> bool:
+        """Loads full character details if not already in memory."""
+        if name in self.ctx.characters:
+            return True
+        try:
+            details = self.data_loader.get_character_details(name)
+            self.ctx.characters[name] = details
+            
+            # Pre-compute tags for the new character if randomizer exists
+            if self.ctx.randomizer:
+                self.ctx.randomizer._precompute_character_tags(name, details)
+                
+            return True
+        except Exception as e:
+            print(f"Error lazy loading character {name}: {e}")
+            return False
+

         """Force clear cache and reload all data."""
         self.data_loader.clear_cache()
+        self.ctx.characters.clear() # Clear full data cache

 """Renderers for converting prompt data into formatted text.
 
 This module provides specialized renderers for different prompt components:
-- OutfitRenderer: Formats outfit dictionaries into text
-- PoseRenderer: Formats pose descriptions
-- SceneRenderer: Formats scene/setting information
-- NotesRenderer: Formats additional notes
-- CharacterRenderer: Formats complete character information
+- OutfitRenderer: Formats outfit dictionaries into text, handling color substitutions and modifiers.
+- PoseRenderer: Formats pose descriptions, including framing.
+- SceneRenderer: Formats scene/setting information.
+- NotesRenderer: Formats additional notes with character name replacement.
+- CharacterRenderer: Formats complete character information, coordinating other renderers.
+- ColorSchemeRenderer: Resolves and formats color schemes.
+- ModifierRenderer: Resolves and formats outfit modifiers.
 
 Each renderer follows a consistent interface with a static render() method.
 """

+    @staticmethod
+    def _build_appearance_text(config: SelectedCharacterConfig, data: CharacterDetails) -> str:
+        """Combine base appearance, traits, and tier-based descriptors."""
+        appearance = data.appearance or ""
+        
+        # Character-level traits
+        char_traits = config.character_traits or []
+        trait_texts = [data.traits[ct] for ct in char_traits if ct in data.traits]
+        
+        if trait_texts:
+            if appearance and not appearance.endswith((".", ";")):
+                appearance += ";"
+            appearance += " " + "; ".join(trait_texts)
+
+        # Tier-based descriptors
+        tier_info = []
+        for field in ["age_presentation", "vibe", "bearing"]:
+            val = getattr(data, field, None)
+            if val:
+                label = field.replace("_", " ").title()
+                tier_info.append(f"{label}: {val}")
+        
+        if tier_info:
+            if appearance and not appearance.endswith((".", ";")):
+                appearance += "."
+            appearance += " " + "; ".join(tier_info) + "."
+            
+        return appearance

+class ModifierRenderer:
+    """Resolves and combinations outfit modifiers."""
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def render(config: SelectedCharacterConfig, outfit_data: Any, shared_modifiers: Dict[str, str]) -> str:
+        """Resolve and combine outfit traits/modifiers."""
+        traits = config.outfit_traits or []
+        if not traits and config.outfit_modifier:
+            traits = [config.outfit_modifier]
+        
+        local_modifiers = {}
+        if isinstance(outfit_data, dict) and "modifiers" in outfit_data:
+            local_modifiers = outfit_data.get("modifiers", {})
+        
+        custom_modifiers = config.custom_modifiers or {}
+        
+        modifier_parts = []
+        for t in traits:
+            if t in local_modifiers:
+                modifier_parts.append(local_modifiers[t])
+            elif t in custom_modifiers:
+                modifier_parts.append(custom_modifiers[t])
+            elif t in shared_modifiers:
+                modifier_parts.append(shared_modifiers[t])
+        
+        return "".join(modifier_parts).strip()
+
+
+class ColorSchemeRenderer:
+    """Resolves color schemes for outfits."""
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def resolve(
+        config: SelectedCharacterConfig, 
+        outfit_data: Any, 
+        shared_schemes: Dict[str, Dict[str, str]],
+        active_team_scheme: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None
+    ) -> Dict[str, str]:
+        """Auto-detect or resolve the color scheme."""
+        scheme_name = config.color_scheme
+        scheme = shared_schemes.get(
+            scheme_name, shared_schemes.get("The Standard", {})
+        )
+
+        requires_team = False
+        if isinstance(outfit_data, str):
+            requires_team = "{team}" in outfit_data
+        elif isinstance(outfit_data, dict):
+            for v in outfit_data.values():
+                if isinstance(v, str) and "{team}" in v:
+                    requires_team = True
+                    break
+        
+        if requires_team and (not scheme_name or scheme_name == "The Standard"):
+            if active_team_scheme:
+                scheme = active_team_scheme
+            else:
+                # Logic for picking team scheme usually belongs in the coordinator/builder
+                # but we return standard as fallback here.
+                pass
+        
+        return scheme if isinstance(scheme, dict) else {}

+    @staticmethod
+    def scan_metadata(content: str, mtime: float = 0.0, file_size: int = 0) -> Dict[str, CharacterMetadata]:
+        """Lightweight scan of character metadata (for gallery/lazy loading)."""
+        metadata = {}
+        parts = _SUBSECTION_RE.split(content)
+        for i in range(1, len(parts), 2):
+            name = parts[i].strip()
+            body = parts[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(parts) else ""
+            
+            # Extracts only what's needed for the gallery
+            photo_match = _PHOTO_RE.search(body)
+            t_match = _TAGS_RE.search(body)
+            v_match = _VIBE_RE.search(body)
+            b_match = _BEARING_RE.search(body)
+            g_match = _GENDER_RE.search(body)
+            
+            a_match = _AGE_PRESENTATION_RE.search(body)
+            
+            def truncate(s):
+                if not s: return None
+                # Take only the first part before a semicolon or comma
+                clean = re.split(r'[;]', s)[0].strip()
+                if len(clean) > 30:
+                    clean = clean[:27] + "..."
+                return clean
+
+            metadata[name] = CharacterMetadata(
+                name=name,
+                photo=photo_match.group(1).strip() if photo_match else None,
+                tags=[t.strip() for t in re.split(r",|;", t_match.group(1))] if t_match else [],
+                vibe=truncate(v_match.group(1)) if v_match else None,
+                bearing=truncate(b_match.group(1)) if b_match else None,
+                age_presentation=truncate(a_match.group(1)) if a_match else None,
+                gender=g_match.group(1).upper() if g_match else "F",
+                mtime=mtime,
+                file_size=file_size
+            )
+        return metadata

+    @staticmethod
+    def _parse_single_character_body(body: str) -> dict:
+        """Parse the body content of a single character section."""
+        # 1. High-level Metadata
+        photo_match = _PHOTO_RE.search(body)
+        photo = photo_match.group(1).strip() if photo_match else None
+
+        app_match = _APPEARANCE_RE.search(body)
+        appearance = app_match.group(1).strip() if app_match else ""
+        
+        # Clean appearance
+        appearance = re.sub(r"(?s)<!--.*?-->", "", appearance).strip()
+        if appearance:
+            lines = [ln for ln in appearance.splitlines() if not re.match(r"^\s*//", ln)]
+            appearance = "\n".join(lines).strip()
+
+        summary_match = _SUMMARY_RE.search(body)
+        summary = summary_match.group(1).strip() if summary_match else None
+
+        # 2. Outfits
+        outfits = CharacterParser._parse_outfits(body)
+
+        # 3. Attributes & Traits (Metadata block)
+        meta = CharacterParser._parse_metadata(body)
+
+        # 4. Post-process outfits (One-piece detection etc)
+        CharacterParser._post_process_outfits(outfits)
+
+        return {
+            "appearance": appearance,
+            "summary": summary,
+            "outfits": outfits,
+            "photo": photo,
+            **meta
+        }

+    @staticmethod
+    def _parse_outfits(body: str) -> dict:
+        """Extract and parse outfits from the character body."""
+        outfits = {}
+        outfit_parts = _OUTFIT_RE.split(body)
+        
+        if len(outfit_parts) > 1:
+            for i in range(1, len(outfit_parts), 2):
+                o_name = outfit_parts[i].strip()
+                o_body = outfit_parts[i + 1] if i + 1 < len(outfit_parts) else ""
+                outfits[o_name] = CharacterParser._parse_outfit_details(o_body)
+        else:
+            # Legacy single-line format: - **Name:** description
+            for m in re.finditer(r"-\s+\*\*([^:]+):\*\*\s*(.+)", body):
+                outfits[m.group(1).strip()] = m.group(2).strip()
+        
+        return outfits
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _parse_outfit_details(o_body: str) -> Union[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
+        """Parse structured items within a single outfit block."""
+        items = {}
+        o_tags = []
+        lines = o_body.splitlines()
+        line_idx = 0
+        
+        # Extract Meta Header (Tags)
+        while line_idx < len(lines):
+            line = lines[line_idx].strip()
+            if not line:
+                line_idx += 1
+                continue
+            tags_m = re.match(r"\*\*Tags:\*\*\s*(.+)", line, re.IGNORECASE)
+            if tags_m:
+                o_tags = [t.strip() for t in re.split(r",|;", tags_m.group(1)) if t.strip()]
+                line_idx += 1
+                continue
+            # Look ahead for items (bullet or key: value)
+            if re.match(r"^\s*[-*]\s+", line) or (":" in line and not line.startswith("//")):
+                break
+            line_idx += 1
+
+        last_key = None
+        last_indent = 0
+        item_found = False
+
+        # Parse Items
+        while line_idx < len(lines):
+            line = lines[line_idx]
+            if not line.strip():
+                line_idx += 1
+                continue
+            
+            # Match bullet or raw key: value
+            m_marker = re.match(r"^\s*([-*])\s+(.*)$", line)
+            
+            if m_marker:
+                item_found = True
+                content = m_marker.group(2)
+                current_indent = len(line) - len(line.lstrip())
+                
+                # Handle Nested Bullets (Indentation logic)
+                if last_key and current_indent > last_indent:
+                    items[last_key] = f"{items[last_key]}\n{line.strip()}"
+                    line_idx += 1
+                    continue
+                
+                last_indent = current_indent
+                key, val = CharacterParser._split_key_val(content)
+                
+                # Collect continuation lines for this item
+                next_idx = line_idx + 1
+                val_lines = [val] if val else []
+                while next_idx < len(lines) and not re.match(r"^\s*[-*]\s+", lines[next_idx]) and not re.match(r"^####\s+", lines[next_idx]):
+                    val_lines.append(lines[next_idx].strip())
+                    next_idx += 1
+                
+                final_val = " ".join(val_lines).strip()
+                if key:
+                    items[key] = final_val
+                    last_key = key
+                line_idx = next_idx
+            else:
+                # [RESILIENCE] Try to parse raw Key: Value if no bullet found but it seems structured
+                if ":" in line and not line.strip().startswith("//"):
+                    key, val = CharacterParser._split_key_val(line.strip())
+                    if key and key.lower() in ["top", "bottom", "outerwear", "footwear", "accessories", "hair/makeup", "makeup", "dress", "suit"]:
+                        item_found = True
+                        items[key] = val
+                        last_key = key
+                line_idx += 1
+
+        if item_found:
+            items["tags"] = o_tags
+            return items
+        return o_body.strip()
 
-        return validated_chars
+    @staticmethod
+    def _split_key_val(text: str) -> tuple:
+        """Split a bullet point or line into key and value."""
+        # 1. Match **Key**: Value or **Key:** Value
+        m = re.match(r"^\s*\*\*(.*?)(?::\s*)?\*\*[:\s]+(.*)$", text)
+        if m:
+            return m.group(1).strip(), m.group(2).strip()
+        
+        # 2. Match Key: Value
+        if ":" in text:
+            parts = text.split(":", 1)
+            # Ensure the key isn't too long (avoid splitting sentences)
+            if len(parts[0]) < 30:
+                return parts[0].strip(), parts[1].strip()
+            
+        return None, text.strip()
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _parse_metadata(body: str) -> dict:
+        """Extract character attributes and traits."""
+        meta = {}
+        
+        # Gender
+        gender_match = _GENDER_RE.search(body)
+        meta["gender"] = gender_match.group(1).upper() if gender_match else "F"
+        meta["gender_explicit"] = bool(gender_match or _MODIFIER_RE.search(body))
+
+        # Modifiers & Signature
+        m_match = _MODIFIER_RE.search(body)
+        meta["modifier"] = m_match.group(1).strip() if m_match else None
+        
+        s_match = _SIGNATURE_COLOR_RE.search(body)
+        meta["signature_color"] = s_match.group(1).strip() if s_match else None
+
+        # Tags
+        t_match = _TAGS_RE.search(body)
+        meta["tags"] = [t.strip() for t in re.split(r",|;", t_match.group(1))] if t_match else []
+
+        # Tiers (Truncated for clean UI/Prompting)
+        def truncate_metadata(s):
+            if not s: return None
+            # Take only the first part before a semicolon
+            clean = re.split(r'[;]', s)[0].strip()
+            if len(clean) > 40: # Slightly more lenient for full details
+                clean = clean[:37] + "..."
+            return clean
+
+        pairs = [
+            ("age_presentation", _AGE_PRESENTATION_RE),
+            ("vibe", _VIBE_RE),
+            ("bearing", _BEARING_RE)
+        ]
+        for key, regex in pairs:
+            m = regex.search(body)
+            meta[key] = truncate_metadata(m.group(1)) if m else None
+
+        # Traits
+        traits = {}
+        tr_match = _TRAITS_RE.search(body)
+        if tr_match:
+            for line in tr_match.group(1).strip().splitlines():
+                line = line.strip()
+                if line.startswith("- "):
+                    content = line[2:].strip()
+                    if ":" in content:
+                        k, v = content.split(":", 1)
+                        traits[k.strip().strip("*")] = v.strip()
+                    else:
+                        traits[content.strip().strip("*")] = content
+        meta["traits"] = traits
+        
+        return meta
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _post_process_outfits(outfits: dict):
+        """Analyze outfits for one-piece detection and other logic."""
+        for name, data in outfits.items():
+            if not isinstance(data, dict): continue
+            
+            # Detect One-Piece
+            bottom_val = None
+            bottom_key = next((k for k in data if k.lower() == "bottom"), None)
+            if bottom_key:
+                bottom_val = str(data[bottom_key]).lower()
+            
+            is_one_piece = not bottom_key or not bottom_val or any(k in bottom_val for k in ["na", "none", "one-piece", "dress", "teddy"])
+            
+            if is_one_piece:
+                data["Bottom"] = None
+                data["one_piece"] = True

     def parse_identity_locks(appearance_text: str) -> dict | None:

+    def get_character_details(self, name: str) -> CharacterDetails:
+        """Load full character details on demand."""
+        if name in self._characters_full:
+            return self._characters_full[name]
+            
+        char_file = self._character_file_map.get(name)
+        if not char_file or not char_file.exists():
+            # Attempt to find it if file map is missing
+            self.load_characters_metadata()
+            char_file = self._character_file_map.get(name)
+            
+        if not char_file:
+            raise ValueError(f"Character '{name}' not found.")
+            
+        try:
+            text = char_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+            file_chars = MarkdownParser.parse_characters(text)
+            if name in file_chars:
+                details = file_chars[name]
+                
+                # Load shared outfits and merge
+                shared_outfits = self.load_outfits()
+                merged_outfits, categorized_outfits = MarkdownParser.merge_character_outfits(
+                    details.dict() if hasattr(details, 'dict') else details, 
+                    shared_outfits, 
+                    name
+                )
+                
+                # Update details with merged outfits
+                details.outfits = merged_outfits
+                details.outfits_categorized = categorized_outfits
+                
+                # Resolve photo
+                resolved = self.resolve_photo_for_character(name, details.photo)
+                details.photo = resolved
+                
+                self._characters_full[name] = details
+                return details
+        except Exception as e:
+            from utils import logger
+            logger.exception(f"Error loading full details for {name}")
+            raise
+            
+        raise ValueError(f"Failed to load details for character '{name}'.")
+
+    def load_characters(self):
+        """Compatibility method for existing code. Loads all characters metadata first."""
+        # For now, we still return a full dict if legacy code expects it, 
+        # but we'll transition to lazy loading in the UI.
+        # If we really want lazy loading, we'd return a proxy or handle it in AppState.
+        
+        # Transition: We'll load metadata first.
+        self.load_characters_metadata()
+        
+        # Legacy behavior: Actually load all of them if needed by other components (e.g. Randomizer)
+        # But for performance, we should ideally refactor consumers.
+        chars = {}
+        for name in self.characters_metadata.keys():
+            chars[name] = self.get_character_details(name)
         return chars

+    def _compute_tag_transitive_closure(self):
+        """Pre-calculate the full expansion of every tag in tag_aliases."""
+        # Start with direct aliases
+        closure = {k.lower(): set(v) for k, v in self.tag_aliases.items()}
+        
+        # Add Mood: tags to closure
+        for k in list(closure.keys()):
+            if k.startswith("mood:"):
+                mood_name = k[5:].strip()
+                if mood_name:
+                    closure[k].add(mood_name)
+
+        # Iterate until no more changes
+        changed = True
+        while changed:
+            changed = False
+            for tag, expanded in closure.items():
+                new_tags = set(expanded)
+                for t in expanded:
+                    t_lower = str(t).lower()
+                    if t_lower in closure:
+                        for target in closure[t_lower]:
+                            if target not in new_tags:
+                                new_tags.add(target)
+                                changed = True
+                closure[tag] = new_tags
+        
+        # Lowercase everything for consistency
+        self._tag_expansion_cache = {k: {str(t).lower() for t in v} for k, v in closure.items()}
+
+    def _index_scenes(self):
+        """Index scenes by their tags for O(1) hub-based lookups."""
+        self._scene_tag_index = {}
+        if not self.scenes:
+            return
+            
+        for cat_name, presets in self.scenes.items():
+            for s_name, s_data in presets.items():
+                tags = self._get_tags(s_data)
+                for t in tags:
+                    t_lower = t.lower()
+                    if t_lower not in self._scene_tag_index:
+                        self._scene_tag_index[t_lower] = []
+                    self._scene_tag_index[t_lower].append((cat_name, s_name, s_data))

+    def get_base64(self, img_data):
+        """Lazy load base64 for web mode."""
+        if not getattr(self.app, 'is_web', False):
+            return None
+        if img_data.get("base64"):
+            return img_data["base64"]
+            
+        try:
+            with open(img_data["path"], "rb") as image_file:
+                # In Web Mode, base64 payload size for large images kills WS. 
+                # Flet doesn't support easy resizing without PIL. We just load it for now since page_size is small.
+                encoded_string = base64.b64encode(image_file.read()).decode("utf-8")
+                img_data["base64"] = encoded_string
+                return encoded_string
+        except Exception as e:
+            return None

+                            reader.onloadend = () => resolve(reader.result);
+                            reader.onerror = reject;

+    async def _wait_for_generation(self, page: Page, timeout_ms: int = 45000):
+        """Wait for generation to complete by watching the UI state."""
+        print(f"Waiting for generation (max {timeout_ms/1000}s)...")
+        start_time = time.time()
+        
+        # Strategy: While the "Stop" button is visible, we are generating.
+        # Or while the "Run" button is disabled.
+        
+        while (time.time() - start_time) * 1000 < timeout_ms:
+            # Check for "Stop" button
+            stop_btn = page.locator('button[aria-label*="Stop"]').first
+            if await stop_btn.count() > 0 and await stop_btn.is_visible():
+                await page.wait_for_timeout(1000)
+                continue
+                
+            # Check for "Run" button becoming enabled
+            run_btn = page.locator('button[aria-label*="Run"]').first
+            if await run_btn.count() > 0:
+                is_disabled = await run_btn.get_attribute("disabled")
+                if is_disabled is not None: # It has a disabled attribute
+                    await page.wait_for_timeout(1000)
+                    continue
+            
+            # Check for error messages or safety blocks
+            if await page.locator("text='Safety'").count() > 0 or await page.locator("text='blocked'").count() > 0:
+                print("Detected potential safety block or error.")
+                break
+
+            # If we don't see a stop button and run is not disabled, maybe it's done?
+            # Let's wait a bit more to be sure
+            await page.wait_for_timeout(2000)
+            break
+            
+        print(f"Generation finished or timed out after {int(time.time() - start_time)}s")
